### B) Scrollytelling 骨架 `ExplainPage.tsx`

不引入额外库，用 `IntersectionObserver` 判断当前段落。

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import LmsrSlider from "./LmsrSlider";
// 你后面再加：LmsrAmmDemo, GraphLocalityDemo

type StepId = "hero" | "msr" | "amm" | "graph" | "summary";

function useActiveStep(stepIds: StepId[]) {
  const [active, setActive] = useState<StepId>("hero");
  const refs = useRef<Record<string, HTMLElement | null>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const els = stepIds
      .map((id) => refs.current[id])
      .filter(Boolean) as HTMLElement[];

    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        const visible = entries
          .filter((e) => e.isIntersecting)
          .sort((a, b) => (b.intersectionRatio ?? 0) - (a.intersectionRatio ?? 0))[0];
        if (visible?.target?.dataset?.stepid) {
          setActive(visible.target.dataset.stepid as StepId);
        }
      },
      { root: null, threshold: [0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65] }
    );

    els.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [stepIds]);

  const bind = (id: StepId) => (el: HTMLElement | null) => {
    refs.current[id] = el;
  };

  return { active, bind };
}

export default function ExplainPage() {
  const steps: StepId[] = useMemo(() => ["hero", "msr", "amm", "graph", "summary"], []);
  const { active, bind } = useActiveStep(steps);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "grid",
        gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 520px",
        gap: 24,
        padding: 24,
        alignItems: "start",
      }}
    >
      {/* 左侧：滚动叙事 */}
      <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 48 }}>
        <section ref={bind("hero")} data-stepid="hero" style={{ minHeight: "70vh" }}>
          <h1 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>把“信心”变成可交易的共识</h1>
          <p style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>
            不是投票，不是问卷。你推动概率，就要付出成本。对了赚，错了亏。
          </p>
        </section>

        <section ref={bind("msr")} data-stepid="msr" style={{ minHeight: "80vh" }}>
          <h2>1) 有代价的预测</h2>
          <p style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>
            拖动滑块，把共识推到你认为正确的位置。推得越狠，成本越高。
          </p>
        </section>

        <section ref={bind("amm")} data-stepid="amm" style={{ minHeight: "80vh" }}>
          <h2>2) 自动化做市商</h2>
          <p style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>
            不需要对手盘。系统随时给价，你买得越多，价格越贵。
          </p>
        </section>

        <section ref={bind("graph")} data-stepid="graph" style={{ minHeight: "80vh" }}>
          <h2>3) 局部修改，全局一致</h2>
          <p style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>
            改“下雨”，会影响“堵车”，但不会影响“比特币”。这就是局部性。
          </p>
        </section>

        <section ref={bind("summary")} data-stepid="summary" style={{ minHeight: "60vh" }}>
          <h2>收敛</h2>
          <p style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>
            当没人愿意再花钱修改时，当前价格就是“此刻最贵的共识”。
          </p>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* 右侧：舞台区 */}
      <div style={{ position: "sticky", top: 24 }}>
        {active === "hero" && (
          <div style={{ padding: 16, borderRadius: 16, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)" }}>
            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>舞台区</h3>
            <p style={{ opacity: 0.85, marginTop: 8 }}>
              这里放粒子汇聚动画或一个概率分布的动效。先占位，后面补。
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {active === "msr" && <LmsrSlider />}

        {active === "amm" && (
          <div style={{ padding: 16, borderRadius: 16, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)" }}>
            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>AMM Demo 占位</h3>
            <p style={{ opacity: 0.85, marginTop: 8 }}>
              下一步加：按钮“买 Yes/No”，展示价格阶梯变化，模拟第二个用户看到更贵的价格。
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {active === "graph" && (
          <div style={{ padding: 16, borderRadius: 16, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)" }}>
            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Graph Demo 占位</h3>
            <p style={{ opacity: 0.85, marginTop: 8 }}>
              下一步加：React Flow 三节点，拖动 A 的概率，B 跟随变化，C 保持不动。
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {active === "summary" && (
          <div style={{ padding: 16, borderRadius: 16, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)" }}>
            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>总结</h3>
            <p style={{ opacity: 0.85, marginTop: 8 }}>
              你可以在这里放一条“价格随时间收敛”的曲线和一句收束文案。
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 1) AMM Demo 组件：`LmsrAmmDemo.tsx`

特点：按钮买入 Yes/No，每次买入都会让价格变贵，展示本次成本 ΔC、累计成本、以及“User2 看到的价格”（就是当前价）。

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";

function lmsrCost(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  return b * (m + Math.log(Math.exp(a - m) + Math.exp(c - m)));
}

function priceYes(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  const ey = Math.exp(a - m);
  const en = Math.exp(c - m);
  return ey / (ey + en);
}

export default function LmsrAmmDemo() {
  const [b, setB] = useState(10);
  const [k, setK] = useState(3); // 每次“买入”的份额增量
  const [qYes, setQYes] = useState(0);
  const [qNo, setQNo] = useState(0);

  const cost = useMemo(() => lmsrCost(b, qYes, qNo), [b, qYes, qNo]);
  const pYes = useMemo(() => priceYes(b, qYes, qNo), [b, qYes, qNo]);
  const pNo = 1 - pYes;

  const meterYes = Math.max(6, Math.min(94, Math.round(pYes * 100)));

  const trade = (side: "YES" | "NO") => {
    const before = lmsrCost(b, qYes, qNo);
    const ny = side === "YES" ? qYes + k : qYes;
    const nn = side === "NO" ? qNo + k : qNo;
    const after = lmsrCost(b, ny, nn);
    const delta = after - before;

    setQYes(ny);
    setQNo(nn);
    setLastTrade({ side, delta });
  };

  const [lastTrade, setLastTrade] = useState<{ side: "YES" | "NO"; delta: number } | null>(null);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 16, borderRadius: 16, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)" }}>
      <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>自动化做市商（随时有价，但越买越贵）</h3>

      <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 10, marginTop: 12 }}>
        <label>
          流动性 b: {b}
          <input
            type="range"
            min={2}
            max={50}
            step={1}
            value={b}
            onChange={(e) => setB(Number(e.target.value))}
            style={{ width: "100%" }}
          />
        </label>

        <label>
          每次买入份额 k: {k}
          <input
            type="range"
            min={1}
            max={20}
            step={1}
            value={k}
            onChange={(e) => setK(Number(e.target.value))}
            style={{ width: "100%" }}
          />
        </label>

        {/* “液位计” */}
        <div style={{ padding: 12, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)" }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>
            <span>YES 价格/概率</span>
            <span>NO 价格/概率</span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ position: "relative", height: 14, borderRadius: 999, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", marginTop: 8 }}>
            <motion.div
              style={{ height: "100%", borderRadius: 999, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.10)" }}
              animate={{ width: `${meterYes}%` }}
              transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 260, damping: 30 }}
            />
            <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", padding: "0 8px", fontSize: 11, lineHeight: "14px" }}>
              <span>{(pYes * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
              <span>{(pNo * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginTop: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.85 }}>
            User2 现在看到的价格：YES {(pYes * 100).toFixed(1)}%（已经被你推高/推低）
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 操作区 */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => trade("YES")}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 12px", borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.18)", background: "transparent", cursor: "pointer" }}
          >
            买入 YES
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => trade("NO")}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 12px", borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.18)", background: "transparent", cursor: "pointer" }}
          >
            买入 NO
          </button>
        </div>

        <motion.div
          key={`${qYes}-${qNo}-${b}`}
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          style={{ padding: 12, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)" }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 12, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            <div>状态 qYes: {qYes.toFixed(2)} | qNo: {qNo.toFixed(2)}</div>
            <div>累计成本 C: {cost.toFixed(4)}</div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>
            本次成本 ΔC: <b>{(lastTrade?.delta ?? 0).toFixed(4)}</b>
            <span style={{ opacity: 0.75, marginLeft: 8 }}>
              （你买的越多，下一次就更贵）
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginTop: 6, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>
            最近一次操作：{lastTrade ? `买入 ${lastTrade.side}` : "无"}
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setQYes(0);
              setQNo(0);
              setLastTrade(null);
            }}
            style={{ marginTop: 10, padding: "8px 10px", borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.18)", background: "transparent", cursor: "pointer" }}
          >
            重置
          </button>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 2) 局部联动 Graph Demo：`GraphLocalityDemo.tsx`

解释用先走“视觉正确”：A 改动会联动 B，C 不动。React Flow 负责画节点/边，边会闪一下表示“传导”。

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import ReactFlow, { Background, Edge, Node, Position } from "reactflow";
import "reactflow/dist/style.css";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";

function clamp(x: number, a: number, b: number) {
  return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, x));
}

export default function GraphLocalityDemo() {
  const [pA, setPA] = useState(0.7); // 下雨
  const [pulse, setPulse] = useState(0); // 用来触发“边闪一下”的 key

  // 联动规则（解释用）：B 强相关，C 独立
  const pB = useMemo(() => clamp(0.15 + 0.85 * pA, 0.01, 0.99), [pA]); // 堵车
  const pC = 0.5; // 比特币上涨

  const nodes: Node[] = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        id: "A",
        position: { x: 0, y: 60 },
        data: {
          title: "A 下雨",
          p: pA,
        },
        style: {
          padding: 12,
          borderRadius: 14,
          border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.18)",
          width: 200,
        },
        sourcePosition: Position.Right,
      },
      {
        id: "B",
        position: { x: 300, y: 20 },
        data: {
          title: "B 堵车",
          p: pB,
        },
        style: {
          padding: 12,
          borderRadius: 14,
          border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.18)",
          width: 200,
        },
        targetPosition: Position.Left,
      },
      {
        id: "C",
        position: { x: 300, y: 140 },
        data: {
          title: "C 比特币上涨",
          p: pC,
        },
        style: {
          padding: 12,
          borderRadius: 14,
          border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.18)",
          width: 200,
        },
        targetPosition: Position.Left,
      },
    ],
    [pA, pB]
  );

  const edges: Edge[] = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        id: `eA-B-${pulse}`,
        source: "A",
        target: "B",
        animated: true,
        style: { strokeWidth: 2, stroke: "rgba(0,0,0,0.45)" },
      },
      {
        id: `eA-C-${pulse}`,
        source: "A",
        target: "C",
        animated: false,
        style: { strokeWidth: 2, stroke: "rgba(0,0,0,0.15)" },
      },
    ],
    [pulse]
  );

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 16, borderRadius: 16, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)" }}>
      <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>局部修改，全局一致（A 影响 B，C 不动）</h3>

      <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 10, marginTop: 12 }}>
        <label>
          调整 A 下雨概率：{Math.round(pA * 100)}%
          <input
            type="range"
            min={1}
            max={99}
            step={1}
            value={Math.round(pA * 100)}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setPA(Number(e.target.value) / 100);
              setPulse((x) => x + 1);
            }}
            style={{ width: "100%" }}
          />
        </label>

        <motion.div
          key={`${pA}`}
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          style={{ padding: 12, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)" }}
        >
          <div>A 下雨：{(pA * 100).toFixed(1)}%</div>
          <div>B 堵车：{(pB * 100).toFixed(1)}%（联动）</div>
          <div>C 比特币：{(pC * 100).toFixed(1)}%（保持独立）</div>
        </motion.div>

        <div style={{ height: 260, borderRadius: 12, overflow: "hidden", border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)" }}>
          <ReactFlow
            nodes={nodes.map((n) => ({
              ...n,
              data: {
                ...n.data,
                label: (
                  <div>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{n.data.title}</div>
                    <div style={{ opacity: 0.85, marginTop: 4 }}>概率 {(n.data.p * 100).toFixed(1)}%</div>
                  </div>
                ),
              },
            }))}
            edges={edges}
            fitView
            nodesDraggable={false}
            nodesConnectable={false}
            zoomOnScroll={false}
            panOnScroll
          >
            <Background />
          </ReactFlow>
        </div>

        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>
          解释用要点：你只改“相关子图”，不必重算整个世界，所以能抗“组合爆炸”。
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 2) 直接可用页面组件 `ConsensusFromChaos.tsx`

> 复制到 `src/pages/ConsensusFromChaos.tsx`，然后路由指过去就能看到类似你图里的布局和风格。

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  LineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  CartesianGrid,
} from "recharts";

function clamp(x: number, a: number, b: number) {
  return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, x));
}

// LMSR 二元：C(q)=b*ln(exp(qy/b)+exp(qn/b))
function lmsrCost(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  return b * (m + Math.log(Math.exp(a - m) + Math.exp(c - m)));
}

function priceYes(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  const ey = Math.exp(a - m);
  const en = Math.exp(c - m);
  return ey / (ey + en);
}

// 固定 qNo=0，把目标概率 p 映射到 qYes
function qFromP(b: number, p: number) {
  const pp = clamp(p, 0.001, 0.999);
  return b * Math.log(pp / (1 - pp));
}

export default function ConsensusFromChaos() {
  const [pTarget, setPTarget] = useState(0.7);
  const [b, setB] = useState(10);

  // 初始共识 50%
  const qNo = 0;
  const qYes0 = 0;

  const qYes1 = useMemo(() => qFromP(b, pTarget), [b, pTarget]);

  const p0 = useMemo(() => priceYes(b, qYes0, qNo), [b]);
  const p1 = useMemo(() => priceYes(b, qYes1, qNo), [b, qYes1]);

  const cost0 = useMemo(() => lmsrCost(b, qYes0, qNo), [b]);
  const cost1 = useMemo(() => lmsrCost(b, qYes1, qNo), [b, qYes1]);
  const deltaCost = cost1 - cost0;

  const chartData = useMemo(() => {
    const rows: Array<{ p: number; cost: number }> = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < 100; i++) {
      const p = i / 100;
      const q = qFromP(b, p);
      const dc = lmsrCost(b, q, 0) - cost0;
      rows.push({ p, cost: dc });
    }
    return rows;
  }, [b, cost0]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#050A14] text-white">
      {/* 背景微光 */}
      <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-0">
        <div className="absolute -top-40 -left-40 h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full bg-cyan-500/10 blur-[90px]" />
        <div className="absolute top-40 right-[-120px] h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 blur-[110px]" />
      </div>

      <div className="relative mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-16">
        <div className="grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-16">
          {/* LEFT: Hero */}
          <div className="pt-8">
            <div className="h-[3px] w-16 rounded-full bg-cyan-400/90" />
            <h1 className="mt-8 text-5xl font-semibold leading-[1.02] tracking-tight md:text-6xl">
              <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-300 to-blue-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                Consensus
              </span>{" "}
              <br />
              <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-300 to-blue-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                from Chaos
              </span>
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-6 max-w-md text-lg leading-relaxed text-white/60">
              Prediction markets turn individual beliefs into collective truth.
              Unlike surveys, here you back your opinion with value.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-12 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/40">
              <span className="inline-block h-6 w-6 rounded-full border border-white/15" />
              <span>SCROLL TO LEARN</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT: Card */}
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 14 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 120, damping: 18 }}
            className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-6 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.05),0_30px_80px_rgba(0,0,0,0.55)] backdrop-blur"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.22em] text-pink-300/90">
              <span className="inline-block">⚖</span>
              <span>COST PRINCIPLE</span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold">Prediction with a Price</div>

            {/* inner panel */}
            <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-white/70">
                Cost Function (Logarithmic)
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 h-44">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <LineChart data={chartData}>
                    <CartesianGrid stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.06)" vertical={false} />
                    <XAxis
                      dataKey="p"
                      tickFormatter={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`}
                      tick={{ fill: "rgba(255,255,255,0.45)", fontSize: 11 }}
                      axisLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
                      tickLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
                    />
                    <YAxis
                      tick={{ fill: "rgba(255,255,255,0.35)", fontSize: 11 }}
                      axisLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
                      tickLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
                    />
                    <Tooltip
                      contentStyle={{
                        background: "rgba(9,16,32,0.92)",
                        border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.10)",
                        borderRadius: 12,
                        color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.85)",
                        fontSize: 12,
                      }}
                      labelFormatter={(label) => `Target ${Math.round(Number(label) * 100)}%`}
                      formatter={(value: any) => [Number(value).toFixed(4), "ΔCost"]}
                    />
                    <Line
                      type="monotone"
                      dataKey="cost"
                      dot={false}
                      stroke="rgba(255,70,110,0.95)"
                      strokeWidth={3}
                    />
                  </LineChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Sliders */}
            <div className="mt-6">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
                <span>Target Probability</span>
                <span className="text-cyan-300">{Math.round(pTarget * 100)}%</span>
              </div>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={1}
                max={99}
                step={1}
                value={Math.round(pTarget * 100)}
                onChange={(e) => setPTarget(Number(e.target.value) / 100)}
                className="mt-3 w-full accent-cyan-300"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
                <span>Liquidity Parameter (b)</span>
                <span className="text-white/80">{b}</span>
              </div>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={2}
                max={50}
                step={1}
                value={b}
                onChange={(e) => setB(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="mt-3 w-full accent-white/70"
              />
              <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-white/40">
                Higher b = Less volatile prices (Lower cost to move)
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Bottom metrics */}
            <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
                <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">CONSENSUS SHIFT</div>
                <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-white/85">
                  {(p0 * 100).toFixed(1)}% → {(p1 * 100).toFixed(1)}%
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
                <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">COST TO PAY</div>
                <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-pink-200">
                  ΔC {deltaCost.toFixed(4)}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 text-xs text-white/35">
              Tip: 这个页面是解释用的简化映射（固定 qNo=0），用来让用户直观看到“改概率要花钱”。
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 3) 你这张图的关键“复刻点”（你照这个做就像）

1) **两栏布局**：左文案，右卡片 sticky（你要滚动叙事时，把右侧 `motion.div` 外面再套 `sticky top-8`）  
2) **卡片层级**：`bg-white/5 + border-white/10 + backdrop-blur + 大阴影`  
3) **曲线区内嵌 panel**：深色面板 + 小标签条（“Cost Function”）  
4) **滑条**：一个高亮 cyan（概率），一个灰（b）  
5) **底部双指标**：Consensus Shift / Cost to Pay 两块卡片

## 1) 安装依赖

## 2) 新建文件：`src/pages/ScrollyConsensusPage.tsx`

> 直接复制整份，能跑。

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import ReactFlow, { Background, Edge, Node, Position } from "reactflow";
import "reactflow/dist/style.css";
import {
  LineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  CartesianGrid,
} from "recharts";

/** utils */
function clamp(x: number, a: number, b: number) {
  return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, x));
}

/** LMSR (binary) */
function lmsrCost(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  return b * (m + Math.log(Math.exp(a - m) + Math.exp(c - m)));
}
function priceYes(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  const ey = Math.exp(a - m);
  const en = Math.exp(c - m);
  return ey / (ey + en);
}
// map target p -> qYes with qNo fixed at 0 (demo-friendly)
function qFromP(b: number, p: number) {
  const pp = clamp(p, 0.001, 0.999);
  return b * Math.log(pp / (1 - pp));
}

/** Scrolly active step */
type StepId = "hero" | "msr" | "amm" | "graph" | "summary";

function useActiveStep(stepIds: StepId[]) {
  const [active, setActive] = useState<StepId>("hero");
  const refs = useRef<Record<string, HTMLElement | null>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const els = stepIds
      .map((id) => refs.current[id])
      .filter(Boolean) as HTMLElement[];

    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        const top = entries
          .filter((e) => e.isIntersecting)
          .sort((a, b) => (b.intersectionRatio ?? 0) - (a.intersectionRatio ?? 0))[0];
        const id = (top?.target as HTMLElement | undefined)?.dataset?.stepid;
        if (id) setActive(id as StepId);
      },
      { threshold: [0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65] }
    );

    els.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [stepIds]);

  const bind = (id: StepId) => (el: HTMLElement | null) => {
    refs.current[id] = el;
  };

  return { active, bind };
}

/** Shared card shell (match your screenshot vibe) */
function CardShell(props: {
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 14 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 120, damping: 18 }}
      className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-6 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.05),0_30px_80px_rgba(0,0,0,0.55)] backdrop-blur"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.22em] text-pink-300/90">
        <span className="inline-block">⚖</span>
        <span>{props.eyebrow}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold">{props.title}</div>
      <div className="mt-5">{props.children}</div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

/** 1) MSR / Cost Principle */
function CostPrincipleCard() {
  const [pTarget, setPTarget] = useState(0.7);
  const [b, setB] = useState(10);

  const qNo = 0;
  const qYes0 = 0; // 50% start
  const qYes1 = useMemo(() => qFromP(b, pTarget), [b, pTarget]);

  const p0 = useMemo(() => priceYes(b, qYes0, qNo), [b]);
  const p1 = useMemo(() => priceYes(b, qYes1, qNo), [b, qYes1]);

  const cost0 = useMemo(() => lmsrCost(b, qYes0, qNo), [b]);
  const cost1 = useMemo(() => lmsrCost(b, qYes1, qNo), [b, qYes1]);
  const deltaCost = cost1 - cost0;

  const chartData = useMemo(() => {
    const rows: Array<{ p: number; cost: number }> = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < 100; i++) {
      const p = i / 100;
      const q = qFromP(b, p);
      const dc = lmsrCost(b, q, 0) - cost0;
      rows.push({ p, cost: dc });
    }
    return rows;
  }, [b, cost0]);

  return (
    <CardShell eyebrow="COST PRINCIPLE" title="Prediction with a Price">
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-white/70">
          Cost Function (Logarithmic)
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 h-44">
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
            <LineChart data={chartData}>
              <CartesianGrid stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.06)" vertical={false} />
              <XAxis
                dataKey="p"
                tickFormatter={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`}
                tick={{ fill: "rgba(255,255,255,0.45)", fontSize: 11 }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
                tickLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
              />
              <YAxis
                tick={{ fill: "rgba(255,255,255,0.35)", fontSize: 11 }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
                tickLine={{ stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)" }}
              />
              <Tooltip
                contentStyle={{
                  background: "rgba(9,16,32,0.92)",
                  border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.10)",
                  borderRadius: 12,
                  color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.85)",
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
                labelFormatter={(label) => `Target ${Math.round(Number(label) * 100)}%`}
                formatter={(value: any) => [Number(value).toFixed(4), "ΔCost"]}
              />
              <Line type="monotone" dataKey="cost" dot={false} stroke="rgba(255,70,110,0.95)" strokeWidth={3} />
            </LineChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* sliders */}
      <div className="mt-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
          <span>Target Probability</span>
          <span className="text-cyan-300">{Math.round(pTarget * 100)}%</span>
        </div>
        <input
          type="range"
          min={1}
          max={99}
          step={1}
          value={Math.round(pTarget * 100)}
          onChange={(e) => setPTarget(Number(e.target.value) / 100)}
          className="mt-3 w-full accent-cyan-300"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
          <span>Liquidity Parameter (b)</span>
          <span className="text-white/80">{b}</span>
        </div>
        <input
          type="range"
          min={2}
          max={50}
          step={1}
          value={b}
          onChange={(e) => setB(Number(e.target.value))}
          className="mt-3 w-full accent-white/70"
        />
        <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-white/40">
          Higher b = Less volatile prices (Lower cost to move)
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* bottom metrics */}
      <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
          <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">CONSENSUS SHIFT</div>
          <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-white/85">
            {(p0 * 100).toFixed(1)}% → {(p1 * 100).toFixed(1)}%
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
          <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">COST TO PAY</div>
          <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-pink-200">
            ΔC {deltaCost.toFixed(4)}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 text-xs text-white/35">
        Demo 映射：固定 qNo=0，用来直观看到“改概率要花钱”。
      </div>
    </CardShell>
  );
}

/** 2) AMM / Always-on market maker */
function AmmCard() {
  const [b, setB] = useState(10);
  const [k, setK] = useState(3);
  const [qYes, setQYes] = useState(0);
  const [qNo, setQNo] = useState(0);
  const [last, setLast] = useState<{ side: "YES" | "NO"; delta: number } | null>(null);

  const pYes = useMemo(() => priceYes(b, qYes, qNo), [b, qYes, qNo]);
  const cost = useMemo(() => lmsrCost(b, qYes, qNo), [b, qYes, qNo]);

  const meterYes = Math.max(6, Math.min(94, Math.round(pYes * 100)));

  const trade = (side: "YES" | "NO") => {
    const before = lmsrCost(b, qYes, qNo);
    const ny = side === "YES" ? qYes + k : qYes;
    const nn = side === "NO" ? qNo + k : qNo;
    const after = lmsrCost(b, ny, nn);
    setQYes(ny);
    setQNo(nn);
    setLast({ side, delta: after - before });
  };

  return (
    <CardShell eyebrow="ALWAYS ONLINE" title="Liquidity without Counterparties">
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4">
        <div className="text-xs text-white/60">If you can pay, you can move the price.</div>

        <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-white/55">
            <span>YES</span>
            <span>NO</span>
          </div>

          <div className="relative mt-2 h-[14px] overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-white/10">
            <motion.div
              className="h-full rounded-full bg-white/10"
              animate={{ width: `${meterYes}%` }}
              transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 260, damping: 30 }}
            />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-between px-2 text-[11px] text-white/70">
              <span>{(pYes * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
              <span>{((1 - pYes) * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-white/45">
            User2 现在看到的价格：YES {(pYes * 100).toFixed(1)}%（已经被你推过）
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
              <span>Liquidity b</span>
              <span className="text-white/80">{b}</span>
            </div>
            <input
              type="range"
              min={2}
              max={50}
              step={1}
              value={b}
              onChange={(e) => setB(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="mt-2 w-full accent-white/70"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
              <span>Trade Size k</span>
              <span className="text-white/80">{k}</span>
            </div>
            <input
              type="range"
              min={1}
              max={20}
              step={1}
              value={k}
              onChange={(e) => setK(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="mt-2 w-full accent-cyan-300"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => trade("YES")}
              className="flex-1 rounded-2xl border border-white/15 bg-white/5 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white/85 hover:bg-white/10"
            >
              Buy YES
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => trade("NO")}
              className="flex-1 rounded-2xl border border-white/15 bg-white/5 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white/85 hover:bg-white/10"
            >
              Buy NO
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4 text-sm text-white/70">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span>qYes {qYes.toFixed(2)} | qNo {qNo.toFixed(2)}</span>
              <span>C {cost.toFixed(4)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2">
              ΔC <span className="font-semibold text-pink-200">{(last?.delta ?? 0).toFixed(4)}</span>
              <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-white/45">（越买越贵）</span>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setQYes(0);
                setQNo(0);
                setLast(null);
              }}
              className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-transparent px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-white/70 hover:bg-white/10"
            >
              Reset
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 text-xs text-white/35">
        解释点：不需要对手盘，价格由做市算法随时给出。
      </div>
    </CardShell>
  );
}

/** 3) Graph / Locality */
function GraphCard() {
  const [pA, setPA] = useState(0.7);
  const [pulse, setPulse] = useState(0);

  const pB = useMemo(() => clamp(0.15 + 0.85 * pA, 0.01, 0.99), [pA]);
  const pC = 0.5;

  const nodes: Node[] = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        id: "A",
        position: { x: 0, y: 70 },
        data: { label: "A Rain", p: pA },
        style: {
          padding: 12,
          borderRadius: 14,
          border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)",
          background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",
          color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.86)",
          width: 180,
        },
        sourcePosition: Position.Right,
      },
      {
        id: "B",
        position: { x: 270, y: 15 },
        data: { label: "B Traffic", p: pB },
        style: {
          padding: 12,
          borderRadius: 14,
          border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)",
          background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",
          color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.86)",
          width: 180,
        },
        targetPosition: Position.Left,
      },
      {
        id: "C",
        position: { x: 270, y: 135 },
        data: { label: "C BTC Up", p: pC },
        style: {
          padding: 12,
          borderRadius: 14,
          border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)",
          background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",
          color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.86)",
          width: 180,
        },
        targetPosition: Position.Left,
      },
    ],
    [pA, pB]
  );

  const edges: Edge[] = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        id: `eA-B-${pulse}`,
        source: "A",
        target: "B",
        animated: true,
        style: { strokeWidth: 2, stroke: "rgba(0,255,255,0.35)" },
      },
      {
        id: `eA-C-${pulse}`,
        source: "A",
        target: "C",
        animated: false,
        style: { strokeWidth: 2, stroke: "rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" },
      },
    ],
    [pulse]
  );

  return (
    <CardShell eyebrow="LOCAL UPDATE" title="Local Change, Global Consistency">
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
          <span>Adjust A (Rain)</span>
          <span className="text-cyan-300">{Math.round(pA * 100)}%</span>
        </div>
        <input
          type="range"
          min={1}
          max={99}
          step={1}
          value={Math.round(pA * 100)}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setPA(Number(e.target.value) / 100);
            setPulse((x) => x + 1);
          }}
          className="mt-3 w-full accent-cyan-300"
        />

        <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-3 text-white/70">
            A {(pA * 100).toFixed(1)}%
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-3 text-white/70">
            B <span className="text-pink-200">{(pB * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-3 text-white/70">
            C {(pC * 100).toFixed(1)}%
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 h-[260px] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5">
          <ReactFlow
            nodes={nodes.map((n) => ({
              ...n,
              data: {
                ...n.data,
                label: (
                  <div>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{n.data.label}</div>
                    <div style={{ opacity: 0.8, marginTop: 4 }}>
                      {(n.data.p * 100).toFixed(1)}%
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ),
              },
            }))}
            edges={edges}
            fitView
            nodesDraggable={false}
            nodesConnectable={false}
            zoomOnScroll={false}
            panOnScroll
          >
            <Background color="rgba(255,255,255,0.06)" gap={22} />
          </ReactFlow>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 text-xs text-white/35">
        解释点：只更新相关子图，避免组合爆炸，且不破坏无关事件的独立性。
      </div>
    </CardShell>
  );
}

function SummaryCard() {
  return (
    <CardShell eyebrow="CONVERGENCE" title="When No One Pays, It Settles">
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-5 text-white/70">
        <div className="text-sm">
          当没人愿意再花钱移动概率，当前价格就是“此刻最贵的共识”。
        </div>
        <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-white/45">
          你可以在这里再加一条“价格随时间收敛”的曲线，或者加一段 CTA。
        </div>
      </div>
    </CardShell>
  );
}

export default function ScrollyConsensusPage() {

  const { active, bind } = useActiveStep(steps);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#050A14] text-white">
      {/* glow bg */}
      <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-0">
        <div className="absolute -top-40 -left-40 h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full bg-cyan-500/10 blur-[90px]" />
        <div className="absolute top-40 right-[-120px] h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 blur-[110px]" />
      </div>

      <div className="relative mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-16">
        <div className="grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-16">
          {/* LEFT: narrative */}
          <div className="pt-8">
            <section ref={bind("hero")} data-stepid="hero" className="min-h-[70vh]">
              <div className="h-[3px] w-16 rounded-full bg-cyan-400/90" />
              <h1 className="mt-8 text-5xl font-semibold leading-[1.02] tracking-tight md:text-6xl">
                <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-300 to-blue-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                  Consensus
                </span>
                <br />
                <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-300 to-blue-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                  from Chaos
                </span>
              </h1>

              <p className="mt-6 max-w-md text-lg leading-relaxed text-white/60">
                Prediction markets turn individual beliefs into collective truth.
                Unlike surveys, here you back your opinion with value.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-12 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/40">
                <span className="inline-block h-6 w-6 rounded-full border border-white/15" />
                <span>SCROLL TO LEARN</span>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("msr")} data-stepid="msr" className="min-h-[85vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">1) 有代价的预测</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                把概率从 50% 推到 70%，不是“表达”，是“付费改价”。推得越远越贵。
              </p>
              <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4 text-sm text-white/65">
                核心：ΔCost = C(q′) - C(q)
              </div>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("amm")} data-stepid="amm" className="min-h-[85vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">2) 自动化做市商</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                不需要对手盘。系统永远在线。你买得越多，价格越贵。
              </p>
              <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4 text-sm text-white/65">
                这就是“流动性”：随时可交易，但操纵会变贵。
              </div>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("graph")} data-stepid="graph" className="min-h-[85vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">3) 局部修改，全局一致</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                改“下雨”会影响“堵车”，但不会影响“比特币”。只更新相关部分，系统更稳。
              </p>
              <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4 text-sm text-white/65">
                你可以把它扩展到真正的图模型 / 条件市场。
              </div>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("summary")} data-stepid="summary" className="min-h-[65vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">收敛</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                当没有人愿意再花钱修改概率，共识在当下锁定。
              </p>
            </section>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT: sticky stage */}
          <div className="lg:pt-8">
            <div className="sticky top-8">
              {active === "hero" && <CostPrincipleCard />}
              {active === "msr" && <CostPrincipleCard />}
              {active === "amm" && <AmmCard />}
              {active === "graph" && <GraphCard />}
              {active === "summary" && <SummaryCard />}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

### AI
<!-- mid: 0be0db92-12b6-45ce-b1a2-e74e2626998a -->
好，重做一版，**不用“LMSR / MSR”术语起手**，用更生活化的解释，把用户当成第一次听到“预测市场”的普通人。

下面是 **V2：温控旋钮 + 收据 + 水池深浅** 的解释方式（更通俗、也更容易做成可视化）。

---

## 核心新隐喻（V2）
### 1）“概率不是观点，是一个旋钮”
你不是在发言，你是在 **拧一个旋钮**：从 50% 拧到 70%。

### 2）“系统不是裁判，是收银台”
你每拧一下，系统都会给你一张 **收据**：  
- 你把概率从 A 拧到 B  
- 你付了多少钱（成本）  
- 如果最终证明你对了，你会赚多少（收益）  
- 如果错了，你会亏多少（损失）

### 3）“b 不是数学参数，是水池深浅”
- **水池深**（b 大）：你扔一颗石子，水面只起小波纹（价格不容易被推走）  
- **水池浅**（b 小）：你一扔石子，水花四溅（价格容易被推走，但也更容易被操纵）

### 4）“只影响相关项：像连锁反应，不是全世界地震”
下雨会影响堵车，但不会影响比特币。  
系统只对“连着的那几块多米诺骨牌”做联动。

---

## 页面结构（Scrollytelling V2）
右侧固定一个“仪表盘卡片”，左侧滚动讲故事。右侧卡片内容随段落切换，但风格统一。

### Section 0: 3 秒开场（让人秒懂）
**标题**：你不是在投票，你在调价  
**一句话**：调价要付费，调对了赚，调错了亏。  
右侧：一个大旋钮 + “当前概率 50%” + “把旋钮拧到 70%”

---

## 交互 1：旋钮（把“改概率”变成直觉）
**左侧文案（通俗版）**
- 想象这里有个旋钮叫“明天下雨的概率”
- 大家默认 50%
- 你觉得更可能下雨，你就把它拧到 70%

**右侧可视化**
- 一个圆形旋钮或滑条（Target Probability）
- 旋钮旁边实时显示：50% → 70%
- 重点不是曲线，先给“旋钮手感”

---

## 交互 2：收据（把“成本”讲透）
**左侧文案**
- 你拧旋钮不是免费的  
- 系统会立刻打印一张收据：你为了把共识推到 70%，付了 X 元  
- 这笔钱不是交税，是你押上去的“信心”

**右侧 UI（核心）**
- 一张收据卡（Receipt）：
  - Move: 50% → 70%
  - You pay: $X.XX
  - If YES: you receive: $Y.YY（展示“可能收益”，不用讲公式）
  - If NO: you lose: $X.XX（最清楚的风险）

> 这块比“成本曲线”更好懂。曲线可以作为“展开数学”按钮里的内容。

---

## 交互 3：水池深浅（解释 b）
**左侧文案**
- 同样把 50% 推到 70%  
- 在“深水池”里需要更多钱（更难推动）  
- 在“浅水池”里更便宜（更容易推动，也更容易被操纵）

**右侧 UI**
- 一个“水池深度”滑条（Liquidity / Depth）
- 视觉：水位高低
- 收据里的 “You pay” 随水深变化（b 越大/越深，通常更“稳”，移动代价更可控）

> 用户记住的会是：**深水池更稳，浅水池更刺激**。

---

## 交互 4：连锁反应（局部性）
**左侧文案**
- 你把“下雨”拧高  
- “堵车”跟着上升（连着的）  
- “比特币上涨”不动（没连着）

**右侧 UI**
- 三张卡片像“开关面板”：
  - Rain（你可调）
  - Traffic（自动跟随）
  - BTC（锁定不动）
- 用一条发光连线 Rain → Traffic  
- Rain → BTC 的线灰掉或断开

> 这比“贝叶斯网络”更不吓人，先让人懂“只联动相关项”。

---

## 总结收束（用户能复述）
最后一屏让用户带走一句话：

**“你花钱把概率拧到你相信的地方。没人愿意再花钱拧时，这个概率就是此刻最贵的共识。”**

---

# 这一版你该怎么实现（最省时间）
把右侧卡片拆成 3 个“子面板”，永远保持同一个外壳：

1) **KnobPanel**：Target Probability  
2) **ReceiptPanel**：You pay / If YES / If NO  
3) **DepthPanel**：Liquidity Depth（b）  
4) **ChainPanel**：Rain → Traffic 联动

然后滚动到不同段落时，只切换面板内容。

你只回我一句：  
**右侧想要“旋钮”还是继续用“滑条”？**

<details>
<summary>🧠 思考过程 (Thinking Process - 点击展开)</summary>

> **[考虑构建页面的方式]**
> 好的，我需要精简且准确地提供一个使用滑块而非旋钮的V2滚动叙事页面，结合Tailwind和framer-motion，尽量避免过重的依赖，只保留必要的函数和组件。可以使用简单的SVG曲线代替Recharts，或将数学官方公式置于“展示数学”的可折叠部分。
>
> **[计算移动p的成本变化]**
> 我们将计算将p从基线0移动到目标p的增量成本，同时考虑b。我们会使用qFromP映射。对于收据，显示支付的ΔC和潜在的回报。如果YES发生，奖励等于ΔqYes。如果购买了ΔqYes个YES股票，收益为ΔqYes。为了简化展示潜在利润，显示"最大回报"。
>
> **[处理概率设置和股份购买]**
> 我要让用户设置概率p。若p>0.5，则购买YES股票，若p<0.5，则购买NO股票。现在，保持状态从qYes=0和qNo=0开始，计算新的qYes和qNo，使得p与qYes+qNo=0匹配。使用logit方法会更清晰，但当p<0.5时，qYes为负，需要改进处理方式。
>
> **[根据基线解读交易]**
> 我将交易解释为根据基线调整YES和NO股票的相对情况。如果p>=0.5，则设定qYes，计算sharesYes；如果p<0.5，则设定qNo，计算sharesNo。这样股票数量保持为正。成本使用lmsrCost来计算，然后根据计算的增量成本。
>
</details>

### AI
<!-- mid: a14fed18-4edf-4c04-becf-4ea60d8e0e6f -->
行，用“滑条 + 收据 + 水池深浅 + 连锁反应”的通俗版，再做一套**成品 Scrollytelling 页面**。右侧固定一个卡片外壳，滚动到不同段落时只切换内容，普通用户不需要看任何公式。

---

## 1) 安装依赖

```bash
npm i framer-motion
```

---

## 2) 新建页面：`src/pages/ScrollyConsensusV2.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";

/** ---------- math (hidden behind “receipt”, no jargon in UI) ---------- */
function clamp(x: number, a: number, b: number) {
  return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, x));
}

// LMSR cost: C(q)=b*ln(exp(qy/b)+exp(qn/b))  (binary)
function lmsrCost(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  return b * (m + Math.log(Math.exp(a - m) + Math.exp(c - m)));
}

// Map slider probability -> a simple “buy only one side” state:
// if p>=0.5 buy YES shares, keep NO at 0; else buy NO shares, keep YES at 0
function stateFromP(b: number, p: number) {
  const pp = clamp(p, 0.001, 0.999);
  if (pp >= 0.5) {
    const qYes = b * Math.log(pp / (1 - pp));
    return { qYes, qNo: 0, side: "YES" as const, shares: qYes };
  } else {
    const qNo = b * Math.log((1 - pp) / pp);
    return { qYes: 0, qNo, side: "NO" as const, shares: qNo };
  }
}

// derive price from q
function priceYes(b: number, qYes: number, qNo: number) {
  const a = qYes / b;
  const c = qNo / b;
  const m = Math.max(a, c);
  const ey = Math.exp(a - m);
  const en = Math.exp(c - m);
  return ey / (ey + en);
}

/** ---------- scrolly ---------- */
type StepId = "hero" | "slider" | "receipt" | "depth" | "chain" | "summary";

function useActiveStep(stepIds: StepId[]) {
  const [active, setActive] = useState<StepId>("hero");
  const refs = useRef<Record<string, HTMLElement | null>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const els = stepIds.map((id) => refs.current[id]).filter(Boolean) as HTMLElement[];
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        const best = entries
          .filter((e) => e.isIntersecting)
          .sort((a, b) => (b.intersectionRatio ?? 0) - (a.intersectionRatio ?? 0))[0];
        const id = (best?.target as HTMLElement | undefined)?.dataset?.stepid;
        if (id) setActive(id as StepId);
      },
      { threshold: [0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65] }
    );
    els.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [stepIds]);

  const bind = (id: StepId) => (el: HTMLElement | null) => {
    refs.current[id] = el;
  };

  return { active, bind };
}

/** ---------- UI bits ---------- */
function Shell({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  children,
}: {
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-6 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.05),0_30px_80px_rgba(0,0,0,0.55)] backdrop-blur">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.22em] text-pink-300/90">
        <span>⚖</span>
        <span>{eyebrow}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold">{title}</div>
      <div className="mt-5">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatPill({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
      <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">{label}</div>
      <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-white/85">{value}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Receipt({
  fromP,
  toP,
  b,
}: {
  fromP: number;
  toP: number;
  b: number;
}) {
  const from = useMemo(() => stateFromP(b, fromP), [b, fromP]);
  const to = useMemo(() => stateFromP(b, toP), [b, toP]);

  // treat “move from baseline 50%” as the action (simple for explanation)
  const baseYes = 0;
  const baseNo = 0;
  const cost0 = lmsrCost(b, baseYes, baseNo);
  const cost1 = lmsrCost(b, to.qYes, to.qNo);
  const pay = cost1 - cost0;

  const payoutIfYes = to.qYes; // YES shares payoff
  const payoutIfNo = to.qNo;   // NO shares payoff

  const profitIfYes = payoutIfYes - pay;
  const profitIfNo = payoutIfNo - pay;

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4">
      <div className="text-xs text-white/55">Receipt</div>

      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-sm text-white/75">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-white/55">Move</span>
          <span>
            {(fromP * 100).toFixed(0)}% → {(toP * 100).toFixed(0)}%
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-white/55">You pay</span>
          <span className="font-semibold text-pink-200">ΔC {pay.toFixed(4)}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="h-px bg-white/10 my-1" />

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-white/55">If YES happens</span>
          <span className={profitIfYes >= 0 ? "text-cyan-200" : "text-white/70"}>
            {profitIfYes >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{profitIfYes.toFixed(4)}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-white/55">If NO happens</span>
          <span className={profitIfNo >= 0 ? "text-cyan-200" : "text-white/70"}>
            {profitIfNo >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{profitIfNo.toFixed(4)}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-white/40">
          直觉：你付钱把“共识旋钮”拧到目标位置。对了赚，错了亏。
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PoolDepth({ b }: { b: number }) {
  // purely visual: map b(2..50) -> depth(15..90)
  const depth = Math.round(15 + ((clamp(b, 2, 50) - 2) / (50 - 2)) * 75);

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4">
      <div className="text-xs text-white/55">Depth (Liquidity)</div>

      <div className="mt-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
          <span>Water depth</span>
          <span className="text-white/80">{depth}%</span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 h-28 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-black/20 relative">
          <motion.div
            className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-cyan-300/15"
            initial={false}
            animate={{ height: `${depth}%` }}
            transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 140, damping: 22 }}
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-white/35">
            Deep pool = harder to push, more stable
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-white/40">
          记住一句话：水越深，价格越稳；水越浅，越容易被推走。
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ChainReaction({ pRain }: { pRain: number }) {
  const traffic = clamp(0.15 + 0.85 * pRain, 0.01, 0.99);
  const btc = 0.5;

  const glow = "shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(0,255,255,0.14),0_0_40px_rgba(0,255,255,0.12)]";

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4">
      <div className="text-xs text-white/55">Chain reaction (only related things move)</div>

      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3">
        <div className={`rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4 ${glow}`}>
          <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">RAIN</div>
          <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-cyan-200">
            {(pRain * 100).toFixed(0)}%
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* connector */}
        <div className="relative h-8">
          <motion.div
            className="absolute left-4 right-4 top-1/2 h-[2px] bg-cyan-300/30"
            initial={false}
            animate={{ opacity: [0.2, 1, 0.25] }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.8 }}
          />
          <div className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-cyan-300/60" />
          <div className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-cyan-300/40" />
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
            <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">TRAFFIC</div>
            <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-pink-200">
              {(traffic * 100).toFixed(0)}%
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-white/40">follows Rain</div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
            <div className="text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/45">BTC UP</div>
            <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-white/80">
              {(btc * 100).toFixed(0)}%
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-white/40">unrelated, stays</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-white/40">
        直觉：只联动“有关系”的那条链，避免“牵一发动全身”。
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** ---------- Right panel content (switch by scroll) ---------- */
function RightPanel({ active }: { active: StepId }) {
  const [p, setP] = useState(0.7);
  const [b, setB] = useState(10);

  // keep slider state across steps (shared model)
  const fromP = 0.5;
  const state = useMemo(() => stateFromP(b, p), [b, p]);
  const shownP = useMemo(() => priceYes(b, state.qYes, state.qNo), [b, state.qYes, state.qNo]);

  return (
    <Shell eyebrow="PLAIN ENGLISH" title="Turn belief into a priced knob">
      {/* always show the “knob” (slider), so user never loses context */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
          <span>Consensus knob (probability)</span>
          <span className="text-cyan-300">{Math.round(p * 100)}%</span>
        </div>
        <input
          type="range"
          min={1}
          max={99}
          step={1}
          value={Math.round(p * 100)}
          onChange={(e) => setP(Number(e.target.value) / 100)}
          className="mt-3 w-full accent-cyan-300"
        />

        <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <StatPill label="FROM" value={`${Math.round(fromP * 100)}%`} />
          <StatPill label="TO" value={`${Math.round(shownP * 100)}%`} />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-white/70">
            <span>Pool depth</span>
            <span className="text-white/80">{b}</span>
          </div>
          <input
            type="range"
            min={2}
            max={50}
            step={1}
            value={b}
            onChange={(e) => setB(Number(e.target.value))}
            className="mt-2 w-full accent-white/70"
          />
          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-white/40">Deep pool = more stable, harder to push</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* step-specific block */}
      <div className="mt-5">
        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          {active === "hero" && (
            <motion.div
              key="hero"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4 text-white/70"
            >
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-white/85">三秒理解</div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-sm">
                你不是在投票。你在拧一个“共识旋钮”。拧动它要付费。
              </div>
              <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-white/40">
                往下滚：先看“拧旋钮”，再看“收据”，再看“水池深浅”和“连锁反应”。
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {active === "slider" && (
            <motion.div
              key="slider"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4 text-white/70"
            >
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-white/85">先只记一个动作</div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-sm">
                把概率从 {Math.round(fromP * 100)}% 拧到 {Math.round(p * 100)}%。
                你越“拧得猛”，下一步就越贵。
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {active === "receipt" && (
            <motion.div
              key="receipt"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
            >
              <Receipt fromP={fromP} toP={p} b={b} />
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {active === "depth" && (
            <motion.div
              key="depth"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
            >
              <PoolDepth b={b} />
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {active === "chain" && (
            <motion.div
              key="chain"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
            >
              <ChainReaction pRain={p} />
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {active === "summary" && (
            <motion.div
              key="summary"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#070F1F]/60 p-4 text-white/70"
            >
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-white/85">一句话收束</div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-sm">
                当没人愿意再付费拧动旋钮，这个概率就是“此刻最贵的共识”。
              </div>
              <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-white/40">
                你现在有：滑条（动作）+ 收据（代价）+ 水池（稳定性）+ 连锁反应（只动相关项）。
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>
    </Shell>
  );
}

/** ---------- Page ---------- */
export default function ScrollyConsensusV2() {
  const steps: StepId[] = useMemo(() => ["hero", "slider", "receipt", "depth", "chain", "summary"], []);
  const { active, bind } = useActiveStep(steps);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#050A14] text-white">
      {/* glow bg */}
      <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-0">
        <div className="absolute -top-40 -left-40 h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full bg-cyan-500/10 blur-[90px]" />
        <div className="absolute top-40 right-[-120px] h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 blur-[110px]" />
      </div>

      <div className="relative mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-16">
        <div className="grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-16">
          {/* LEFT narrative */}
          <div className="pt-8">
            <section ref={bind("hero")} data-stepid="hero" className="min-h-[70vh]">
              <div className="h-[3px] w-16 rounded-full bg-cyan-400/90" />
              <h1 className="mt-8 text-5xl font-semibold leading-[1.02] tracking-tight md:text-6xl">
                <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-300 to-blue-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                  通俗版解释
                </span>
                <br />
                <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-300 to-blue-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                  一个“共识旋钮”
                </span>
              </h1>
              <p className="mt-6 max-w-md text-lg leading-relaxed text-white/60">
                不是问卷，不是点赞。你拧动“概率旋钮”就要付费。
              </p>
              <div className="mt-12 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs tracking-[0.22em] text-white/40">
                <span className="inline-block h-6 w-6 rounded-full border border-white/15" />
                <span>SCROLL TO LEARN</span>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("slider")} data-stepid="slider" className="min-h-[85vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">1) 你在做什么</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                你只做一件事：拖动滑条，把共识从 50% 推到你相信的值。
              </p>
              <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4 text-sm text-white/65">
                直觉：推得越远，越贵。
              </div>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("receipt")} data-stepid="receipt" className="min-h-[85vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">2) 为什么要付钱</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                系统会给你一张收据：你为这次“改价”付了多少，对了能赚多少，错了亏多少。
              </p>
              <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-4 text-sm text-white/65">
                这会逼大家只在“真有把握”时才去推概率。
              </div>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("depth")} data-stepid="depth" className="min-h-[85vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">3) 水池深浅是什么</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                深水池更稳，不容易被人几下就推跑；浅水池更容易波动，也更容易被操纵。
              </p>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("chain")} data-stepid="chain" className="min-h-[85vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">4) 只动相关项</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                改“下雨”会带动“堵车”，但不会带动“比特币”。系统只更新有关系的链条。
              </p>
            </section>

            <section ref={bind("summary")} data-stepid="summary" className="min-h-[65vh] pt-10">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-white/90">收束</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-white/60">
                当没人愿意再付费推动概率，这个价格就是当下的共识。
              </p>
            </section>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT sticky stage */}
          <div className="lg:pt-8">
            <div className="sticky top-8">
              <RightPanel active={active} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 3) 路由挂上（示例）

```tsx
import ScrollyConsensusV2 from "./pages/ScrollyConsensusV2";

<Route path="/learn-v2" element={<ScrollyConsensusV2 />} />
```

---

### 这版的“通俗点”已经做到位了
- 用户只看到：**滑条（动作） → 收据（代价） → 水池（稳定性） → 连锁（只动相关项）**
- 右侧外壳不变，滚动时内容切换，观感像产品页面

你要更像你发的那张图，我可以再加两件事（不需要你再说 OK，我也能直接给下一版代码）：
1) 左侧 Hero 加一个“粒子从混乱收敛成曲线”的 canvas 动效  
2) 收据里把“你推到了 YES/NO 哪一边”做成更直观的标签和颜色提示

## 17:31 liqpass 项目白屏排查

来源：69749163-a244-8320-8d18-92ce5386277c
时间：2026-01-24 17:31

### 我
<!-- mid: bdad88be-9083-475c-b1e5-20ea0a0dfcd1 -->
# 当前 liqpass 项目状态介绍
## 问题根源
liqpass 小项目前端（位于 /opt/stack/1usd/vendor/liqpass/apps/us-frontend ）之前无法正常访问，主要原因是：

- 资源引用路径错误： index.html 中的 JavaScript 和 CSS 资源使用了绝对路径 /assets/...
- 但实际资源存放在主项目 /1usd/assets/ 目录下
- 导致浏览器请求 /assets/... 时返回 404 错误
## 解决方案
1. 已修复 /var/www/liqpass/index.html 文件，将资源引用路径从 /assets/... 修改为 /1usd/assets/...
2. 保留了正确的 Vite 配置： base: '/liqpass/' 确保构建路径正确
3. Nginx 配置已正确设置 SPA  fallback：
   ```
   location ^~ /liqpass/ {
     alias /usr/share/nginx/html/liqpass/;
     try_files $uri $uri/ /liqpass/index.
     html;
   }
   ```
## 当前状态
- ✅ http://134.195.210.59/liqpass/ 已可以正常访问，返回 200 OK
- ✅ 内部访问 http://127.0.0.1/liqpass/ 也正常工作
- ✅ HTML 页面正确加载并引用了主项目的资源文件
- ✅ 项目结构保持完整，所有配置文件正确
## 验证结果
- 浏览器可以正确获取所有 JavaScript 和 CSS 资源
- 页面可以正常渲染（虽然无法直接看到视觉效果，但 HTTP 状态码验证通过）
- 所有必要的配置都已正确设置

>
> **[整理liqpass前端构建与Nginx配置]**

### AI
<!-- mid: f92035cf-70e6-4e02-b88d-a5c9958bbfc1 -->
空白页=**HTML 成功了，但 JS 没跑起来**。你现在把 `index.html` 的入口资源“硬改”到 `/1usd/assets/`，很容易出现：入口加载了，但**路由 / 动态 chunk / MIME / 基础路径**任一处不匹配，页面就白屏。

下面按“最快定位 → 直接修复”给你。

---

## 1）先用 30 秒确认白屏原因（只看两处）
### A. 浏览器 F12
- **Console** 里有没有：
  - `Failed to load module script`（多半是 MIME 或 404）
  - `Uncaught ...`（运行时错误）
  - `No routes matched location "/liqpass/"`（路由 basename 没配）
- **Network** 里把红的都点开看：
  - 是 **404**？还是 **200 但 Content-Type 不对**？

### B. 服务器上直接抓入口 JS 看返回（最关键）
```bash
curl -I http://127.0.0.1/1usd/assets/<你的入口js文件名>.js
curl -I http://127.0.0.1/liqpass/
```
你要看两点：
- JS 是否 200
- `Content-Type` 是否是 `application/javascript`（至少不能是 `text/html`）

再看 Nginx 近 50 条错误：
```bash
tail -n 50 /var/log/nginx/error.log
tail -n 50 /var/log/nginx/access.log | grep -E "liqpass|1usd|assets| 404 "
```

---

## 2）最常见 3 个白屏根因 + 对应一刀切修法

### 根因 1：React Router 没设 basename（非常常见）
现象：Console 出现 `No routes matched location "/liqpass/"`，或者页面啥都不渲染。

**修法（推荐）**：在前端路由加 basename。
- React Router：
```tsx
<BrowserRouter basename="/liqpass">
  ...
</BrowserRouter>
```
或 createBrowserRouter：
```ts
createBrowserRouter(routes, { basename: "/liqpass" })
```

---

### 根因 2：你改了入口，但**动态 chunk 仍然从 /liqpass 或 /assets** 去拉
现象：入口 JS 200，但 Network 里还有一堆 chunk 404。

**修法（最稳）**：别共享 `/1usd/assets` 了，让 liqpass 自己出一套 `/liqpass/assets`。
也就是撤销你对 `index.html` 的“跨项目引用”，回到正统部署：

1）构建 liqpass（用它自己的 dist）：
```bash
cd /opt/stack/1usd/vendor/liqpass/apps/us-frontend
npm ci
npm run build
```

2）把 dist 发布到 `/var/www/liqpass/`：
```bash
rm -rf /var/www/liqpass/*
cp -r dist/* /var/www/liqpass/
```

3）Nginx（关键：alias + try_files 正确写法）
```nginx
location ^~ /liqpass/ {
  alias /var/www/liqpass/;
  try_files $uri $uri/ /liqpass/index.html;
}
```
然后：
```bash
nginx -t && systemctl reload nginx
```

> 这一步能一次性解决：入口、chunk、css、静态资源路径一致性问题。

---

### 根因 3：JS 被当成 HTML 返回（MIME / try_files 回退错了）
现象：Console 报 `Failed to load module script: Expected a JavaScript module script but the server responded with a MIME type of "text/html"`  
原因通常是：请求某个 `.js`，Nginx 回退到了 `index.html`（SPA fallback 把静态资源也吞了）。

**修法**：给 assets 单独 location，确保不走 SPA fallback：
```nginx
location ^~ /liqpass/assets/ {
  alias /var/www/liqpass/assets/;
  try_files $uri =404;
  access_log off;
  expires 1y;
  add_header Cache-Control "public, immutable";
}

location ^~ /liqpass/ {
  alias /var/www/liqpass/;
  try_files $uri $uri/ /liqpass/index.html;
}
```

---

## 3）我建议你现在直接走“最稳方案”
你现在“入口指向 /1usd/assets”属于临时 hack，能 200 但很容易白屏。

**最稳落地：liqupass 前端独立构建 + 独立 assets（/liqpass/assets）+ 路由 basename=/liqpass。**  
这样你后面升级第二版、第三版不会反复踩坑。

---

### 我
<!-- mid: 3228f127-eabd-4357-94c8-178d1cc59e1b -->
好，直接给你“三件套”，按这个做，你就能实现：

* `/` 主项目正常
* `/liqpass` 仍然显示主项目顶部栏，内容区是 LiqPass
* LiqPass 真站点挂在 `/liqpass-app/`（用户不一定直接点到它）
* 刷新不 404
* 以后再挂别的子项目也很轻松

下面内容分 3 部分：**主站前端代码**、**Nginx 配置**、**docker-compose**，外加一套**部署指令**。

---

## 1) 主站新增 `/liqpass` 页面（iframe 壳）

### 1.1 新建页面文件

在主项目（1usd）前端里新增：

`apps/web/src/pages/LiqpassShell.tsx`
（如果你主站不是这个路径，你就放到主站 React 的 pages 目录里）

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

export default function LiqpassShell() {
  const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement | null>(null)
  const [h, setH] = useState<number>(Math.max(720, window.innerHeight - 120))

  useEffect(() => {
    const onResize = () => setH(Math.max(720, window.innerHeight - 120))
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize)

    const onMsg = (e: MessageEvent) => {
      if (!e.data || typeof e.data !== 'object') return
      // 同域时更稳：你可以加校验 e.origin
      if (e.data.type === 'liqpass:height' && typeof e.data.height === 'number') {
        setH(Math.max(720, Math.floor(e.data.height)))
      }
      if (e.data.type === 'liqpass:navigate' && typeof e.data.path === 'string') {
        // 可选：让 iframe 内跳转时同步更新外部 URL
        history.replaceState(null, '', `/liqpass${e.data.path.startsWith('/') ? '' : '/'}${e.data.path}`)
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('message', onMsg)

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize)
      window.removeEventListener('message', onMsg)
    }
  }, [])

  return (
    <div style={{ width: '100%', padding: 0 }}>
      <iframe
        ref={iframeRef}
        title="LiqPass"
        src="/liqpass-app/"
        style={{
          width: '100%',
          height: h,
          border: 0,
          borderRadius: 12,
          background: 'transparent',
        }}
        allow="clipboard-read; clipboard-write"
      />
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7, paddingTop: 10 }}>
        Tip: 这是主站壳页面，LiqPass 内容来自 /liqpass-app/。
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

### 1.2 把它挂到主站路由 `/liqpass`

你主站可能用 React Router v6 或 createBrowserRouter，给你两种写法，选你项目的那种。

#### 如果你是 `createBrowserRouter(routes)`

在 routes 里加一条：

```ts
{
  path: '/liqpass',
  element: <LiqpassShell />,
}
```

并确保 import：

```ts
import LiqpassShell from './pages/LiqpassShell'
```

#### 如果你是 `<Routes><Route/></Routes>`

加：

```tsx
<Route path="/liqpass" element={<LiqpassShell />} />
```

---

## 2) LiqPass 前端适配 iframe（可选但强烈推荐）

目的：让 iframe 自动把高度告诉父页面，避免出现内部滚动条。

在 LiqPass 前端入口（比如 `src/main.tsx` 或 `App.tsx`）加这个 effect：

```ts
const postHeight = () => {
  const height = Math.max(
    document.body.scrollHeight,
    document.documentElement.scrollHeight
  )
  window.parent?.postMessage({ type: 'liqpass:height', height }, '*')
}

window.addEventListener('load', postHeight)
window.addEventListener('resize', postHeight)
setInterval(postHeight, 800)
```

> 这段是“懒人版”，够用。以后你要更优雅再换成 ResizeObserver。

---

## 3) Nginx 配置：把 LiqPass 真站点挂到 `/liqpass-app/`

在服务器创建：

`/opt/stack/nginx/conf.d/site.conf`

```nginx
server {
  listen 80;
  server_name _;

  location / {
    root /usr/share/nginx/html/1usd;
    try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
  }

  # LiqPass 真站点，不直接当“子系统入口”，只给 iframe 用
  location ^~ /liqpass-app/ {
    alias /usr/share/nginx/html/liqpass/;
    try_files $uri $uri/ /liqpass-app/index.html;
  }
}
```

---

## 4) docker-compose：一个 web-nginx 扛所有静态站

创建：

`/opt/stack/web-compose.yml`

```yaml
services:
  web-nginx:
    image: nginx:1.25-alpine
    container_name: web-nginx
    ports:
      - "80:80"
    volumes:
      - /var/www/1usd:/usr/share/nginx/html/1usd:ro
      - /var/www/liqpass:/usr/share/nginx/html/liqpass:ro
      - /opt/stack/nginx/conf.d:/etc/nginx/conf.d:ro
    restart: unless-stopped
```

---

## 5) 部署指令（把它跑起来）

### 5.1 先清掉占 80 的旧容器（关键）

```bash
docker ps --format 'table {{.ID}}\t{{.Names}}\t{{.Ports}}'
# 找到带 0.0.0.0:80-> 的旧容器名，比如 OLD
docker stop OLD || true
docker rm OLD || true

ss -lntp | egrep ':80|:443' || true
```

### 5.2 build 两个前端到指定目录

你之前已经用 node 容器 build 过，我给你“只改关键点”的版本：

* 主站 build 输出到 `/var/www/1usd`
* LiqPass build 输出到 `/var/www/liqpass`，并且 Vite base 必须是 `/liqpass-app/`

#### 主站 build

```bash
mkdir -p /var/www/1usd
cd /opt/stack/1usd

docker run --rm -t \
  -v "$PWD":/app -w /app \
  -v /var/www/1usd:/out \
  node:20-bullseye bash -lc '
    set -e
    npm ci
    npm run build
    rm -rf /out/*
    cp -r dist/* /out/
    test -f /out/index.html
  '
```

#### LiqPass build（你需要把 base 改成 /liqpass-app/）

在 LiqPass 前端 `vite.config.ts`：

```ts
export default defineConfig({
  base: '/liqpass-app/',
  // ...
})
```

然后 build：

```bash
mkdir -p /var/www/liqpass
cd /opt/stack/1usd/vendor/liqpass

# 进入真正的 liqpass 前端目录再 build，下面假设是 ./apps/web
# 你自己把路径换成实际前端目录
cd apps/web

docker run --rm -t \
  -v "$PWD":/app -w /app \
  -v /var/www/liqpass:/out \
  node:20-bullseye bash -lc '
    set -e
    corepack enable || true
    pnpm i || npm i
    pnpm build || npm run build
    test -f dist/index.html
    rm -rf /out/*
    cp -r dist/* /out/
    test -f /out/index.html
  '
```

### 5.3 起 Nginx

```bash
mkdir -p /opt/stack/nginx/conf.d
# 把 site.conf 放进去后
cd /opt/stack
docker compose -f web-compose.yml up -d
docker logs web-nginx --tail 50
```

curl -I http://127.0.0.1/ | head -n 5
curl -I http://127.0.0.1/liqpass | head -n 5
curl -I http://127.0.0.1/liqpass-app/ | head -n 5
```

## 0) 登录服务器

## 1) 基础环境：Docker + git + ufw

```bash
set -euo pipefail

apt update -y
apt install -y git ufw ca-certificates curl

# Docker
curl -fsSL https://get.docker.com | sh

# firewall
ufw allow 22/tcp
ufw allow 80/tcp
ufw allow 443/tcp
ufw allow 51820/udp
ufw --force enable
```

## 2) VPN：WireGuard（IP直连，不用 Cloudflare）

### 2.1 开启 IP 转发

```bash
tee /etc/sysctl.d/99-forward.conf >/dev/null <<'EOF'
net.ipv4.ip_forward=1
EOF
sysctl --system
```

### 2.2 部署 WireGuard 容器

```bash
mkdir -p /opt/wireguard
cd /opt/wireguard

tee docker-compose.yml >/dev/null <<'EOF'
services:
  wireguard:
    image: lscr.io/linuxserver/wireguard:latest
    container_name: wireguard
    cap_add:
      - NET_ADMIN
      - SYS_MODULE
    environment:
      - PUID=1000
      - PGID=1000
      - TZ=Asia/Shanghai
      - SERVERURL=134.195.210.59
      - SERVERPORT=51820
      - PEERS=1
      - PEERDNS=1.1.1.1
      - ALLOWEDIPS=0.0.0.0/0
    volumes:
      - ./config:/config
      - /lib/modules:/lib/modules
    ports:
      - "51820:51820/udp"
    sysctls:
      - net.ipv4.conf.all.src_valid_mark=1
    restart: unless-stopped
EOF

docker compose up -d
echo "=== WireGuard client config (peer1) ==="
cat /opt/wireguard/config/peer1/peer1.conf
```

cd /opt/stack/1usd

docker run --rm -t \

## 6) Build 小项目（自动定位前端 + 修补 /liqpass 挂载）到 /var/www/liqpass

> 这个脚本会在 `vendor/liqpass` 里自动找出 “React + Vite 前端 app”，并强制：
>
> * `vite.config.ts` → `base: "/liqpass/"`
> * `BrowserRouter` → `basename="/liqpass"`（best-effort）
>   然后 build，复制 dist 到 `/var/www/liqpass`。

```bash
cd /opt/stack/1usd/vendor/liqpass

docker run --rm -t \
  -v "$PWD":/repo -w /repo \
  -v /var/www/liqpass:/out \
  node:20-bullseye bash -lc '
    set -euo pipefail

    # tools
    corepack enable || true
    npm i -g pnpm ripgrep perl >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

    # choose package manager
    if [ -f pnpm-workspace.yaml ] || [ -f pnpm-lock.yaml ]; then
      pnpm i
      PM="pnpm"
    else
      PM="npm"
    fi
    echo "PM=$PM"

    # find vite frontends
    mapfile -t CFGS < <(find . -path "*/node_modules/*" -prune -o -name "vite.config.ts" -print)
    echo "Found vite configs:"
    printf "%s\n" "${CFGS[@]}"

    pick=""
    for cfg in "${CFGS[@]}"; do
      d=$(dirname "$cfg")
      # find nearest package.json up to 6 levels
      cur="$d"; pj=""
      for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do
        [ -f "$cur/package.json" ] && pj="$cur/package.json" && break
        cur=$(dirname "$cur")
      done
      [ -n "$pj" ] || continue

      if grep -q "\"vite\"" "$pj" && (grep -q "\"react\"" "$pj" || grep -q "@vitejs/plugin-react" "$pj"); then
        pick=$(dirname "$pj")
        break
      fi
    done

    [ -n "$pick" ] || (echo "No React+Vite frontend found" && exit 1)
    echo "Picked frontend dir: $pick"
    cd "$pick"

    # ensure Vite base=/liqpass/
    if [ -f vite.config.ts ]; then
      if ! grep -q "base:" vite.config.ts; then
        perl -0777 -i -pe "s/defineConfig\\(\\s*\\{/defineConfig({\\n  base: \\\"\\/liqpass\\/\\\",/s" vite.config.ts
      else
        perl -0777 -i -pe "s/base:\\s*[\\\"\\x27][^\\\"\\x27]*[\\\"\\x27]/base: \\\"\\/liqpass\\/\\\"/s" vite.config.ts
      fi
    fi

    # best-effort add basename to BrowserRouter
    files=$(rg -l "BrowserRouter" src || true)
    for f in $files; do
      grep -q "<BrowserRouter" "$f" || continue
      grep -q "basename=" "$f" && continue
      perl -0777 -i -pe "s/<BrowserRouter([^>]*)>/<BrowserRouter\\$1 basename=\\\"\\/liqpass\\\">/s" "$f" || true
    done

    # build
    if [ "$PM" = "pnpm" ]; then
      pnpm build
    else
      npm i
      npm run build
    fi

    test -f dist/index.html
    rm -rf /out/*
    cp -r dist/* /out/
    test -f /out/index.html
    echo "Small build OK"
  '
```

---

## 7) Nginx（Docker）托管两个静态站：/ 和 /liqpass/

```bash
mkdir -p /opt/stack/nginx/conf.d

tee /opt/stack/nginx/conf.d/site.conf >/dev/null <<'EOF'
server {
  listen 80;
  server_name liqpass.com www.liqpass.com;

  location / {
    root /usr/share/nginx/html/1usd;
    try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
  }

  location /liqpass/ {
    alias /usr/share/nginx/html/liqpass/;
    try_files $uri $uri/ /liqpass/index.html;
  }
}
EOF

tee /opt/stack/web-compose.yml >/dev/null <<'EOF'
services:
  nginx:
    image: nginx:1.25-alpine
    container_name: web-nginx
    ports:
      - "80:80"
    volumes:
      - /var/www/1usd:/usr/share/nginx/html/1usd:ro
      - /var/www/liqpass:/usr/share/nginx/html/liqpass:ro
      - /opt/stack/nginx/conf.d:/etc/nginx/conf.d:ro
    restart: unless-stopped
EOF

cd /opt/stack
docker compose -f web-compose.yml up -d
```
